# Skills · P · part 247

801 entries, prototype-init to prove-it. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prototype-init**: Scaffold a local static prototype site and launch it at localhost:3000. Creates the directory structure that design-wireframes and other prototyping skills write into. Trigger when the user says "prototype-init", "scaffold the prototype", "set up the prototype site", wants to start rapid prototyping from a PRD, or is about to invoke design-wireframes for the first time on a project. Also trigger proactively when a PRD is approved and downstream wireframe/visual design work is about to begin and no `prototype/` directory exists yet. ([aminianp/skills](https://github.com/aminianp/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype-init))
- **prototype-integrator**: Absorb an external AI-generated code file, zip, JSX component, or folder into the real Gloobal codebase. Use whenever code arrives from ChatGPT, Claude.ai, Cursor, v0, Bolt, Lovable, or any tool outside this repo — triggers include "integrate this", "merge this into our project", "the founder shared this file", "add this feature from this file", "port this component", "use this as reference", "take the design from this". Treats external files as design prototypes, never as production-ready code to drop in. Extracts intent, maps to the real codebase, implements safely, verifies with Playwright. ([gloobal-pay-gloobal/Gloobal](https://github.com/gloobal-pay-gloobal/Gloobal/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype-integrator))

- **prov**: PROV: Provenance is information about entities, activities, and people involved in producing a piece of data or thing, which can be used to form assessments about its quality, reliability or trustworthiness. Covers PROV-DM: The PROV Data Model, PROV-O: The PROV Ontology, PROV-N: The Provenance Notation. Use when recording provenance. Triggers: PROV, PROV-O, PROV-DM. ([ScaleDockHQ/scaledock-skills](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/scaledock-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prov) · ★2)
- **prov**: Use when the user asks about code provenance — "why does this code do X", "what was the original prompt for this line", "who or what wrote this", "what's the history of this file", "find the prompts where we decided Y", "has this been rewritten and what came before", "was this AI-written, by which model, in which session". Calls `prov log &lt;file&gt;[:&lt;line&gt;]` to surface the originating prompt, model, conversation, and drift state, and `prov search &lt;query&gt;` to find prompts by content. - **prove-it**: Use when judging whether work is done, or backing a claim. ([MalasataXD/unfuss](https://github.com/MalasataXD/unfuss/tree/HEAD/skills/knowledge/prove-it))
- **prove-it**: Audit a security or privacy claim by probing the running system rather than reading the code — sentinel values, every-route sweeps, real artifacts opened and read. Use for "is that secure?", "prove it", "can anyone see X?", "does that leak?", or before writing any claim into SECURITY.md or the privacy page. ([MichaelMurguia/Production_Stills_App](https://github.com/MichaelMurguia/Production_Stills_App/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prove-it))
- **prove-it**: Forces an AI agent to produce real evidence before claiming a task is complete. Use at the start of any session where the agent will write files, run commands, push to external services, call APIs, or delegate to subagents. Covers ten named false-completion patterns, a claim taxonomy mapping each claim type to its required evidence type, and a verification protocol the agent must follow before any completion statement. ([Nagacash/prove-it](https://github.com/Nagacash/prove-it/tree/HEAD/skills/prove-it))
